TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:57:  // For every "boid" - is "upstairs" . . .  //for each boid (boid):
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:59:      // Zero all accumulator variables
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:63:      // For every other boid in the flock . . .
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:64:      //for each other boid (otherboid):
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:70:          // Compute differences in x,y,z coordinates
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:75:          // Are all those differences less than the visual range?
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:78:              // If so, calculate the squared distance
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:81:              // Is squared distance less than the protected range?
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:84:                  // If so, calculate difference in x/y/z-coordinates to nearfield boid
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:89:              // If not in protected range, is the boid in the visual range?
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:92:                      // Add other boid's x/y/z-coord and x/y/z vel to accumulator variables
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:101:                      // Increment number of boids within visual range
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:107:      // If there were any boids in the visual range . . .            
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:110:          // Divide accumulator variables by number of boids in visual range
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:119:          // Add the centering/matching contributions to velocity
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:133:      // Add the avoidance contribution to (x,y,z)-velocity
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:138:      // If the boid is near an edge, make it turn by turnfactor
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:139:      // but in proportion to the distance from the permitted area
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:155:      // Calculate the boid's speed
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:156:      // Slow step! Lookup the "alpha max plus beta min" algorithm
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:159:      // Enforce min and max speeds
TASK14_Boids_ABM_selforganistion/boids06/birdslogic.pde:174:      // Update boid's position - is in worldphysics!

TASKZZ_GA/GAtools.pde:111:/// To treat an int as unsigned in the context of Gray code, two things must be handled:
TASKZZ_GA/GAtools.pde:112:/// - During encoding to Gray code (long -> int) – truncate upper 32 bits so XOR operations 
TASKZZ_GA/GAtools.pde:113:///   do not include garbage outside the range.
TASKZZ_GA/GAtools.pde:114:/// - During decoding (int -> long) – treat bits from int as a pure positive value 
TASKZZ_GA/GAtools.pde:115:///   (strip sign extension) and then perform decoding.

TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:16:/// @}
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:20:/// Jednowymiarowa Funkcja Rastrigina.
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:21:/// @details 
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:22:/// Wzór matematyczny dla jednej zmiennej wygląda następująco:
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:23:///      \(f(x)=10+x^{2}-10\cdot \cos (2\pi x)\)
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:24:///      f(X) = 10 + x² - 10cos(2πx)
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:25:/// * Zalecany dziedzinowy przedział poszukiwań to `x in [-5.12, 5.12]`. 
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:26:///   W tym przedziale funkcja ma jedno wyraźne minimum globalne w punkcie `x = 0`,
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Gray/GA_Gray.pde:27:///   gdzie wartość wynosi `0`, oraz kilkanaście minimów lokalnych („dolin”, w których

TASKZZ_GA/GA_NKB/GA_NKB.pde:29:/// * Maksimum globalne (Szczyt): Wynosi około 40.35 dla punktów \(x \approx \pm 4.523\).

TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:16:/// @}
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:20:/// One-dimensional Rastrigin Function.
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:21:/// @details 
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:22:/// The mathematical formula for a single variable is as follows:
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:23:///      \(f(x)=10+x^{2}-10\cdot \cos (2\pi x)\)
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:24:///      f(X) = 10 + x² - 10cos(2πx)
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:25:/// * Recommended domain search interval is `x in [-5.12, 5.12]`. 
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:26:///   In this interval, the function has one clear global minimum at point `x = 0`,
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:27:///   where the value is `0`, and multiple local minima ("valleys", in which 
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:28:///   you can observe the algorithm getting stuck if the mutation rate is too small).
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:29:/// * Global minimum (Bottom): Exactly 0.0 at point `x = 0`.
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:30:/// * Global maximum (Peak): Approximately 40.35 at points \(x \approx \pm 4.523\).
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:31:/// * cf.: https://www.wikiwand.com/en/Rastrigin_function . There are also links to 
TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:32:///   other interesting benchmark functions for optimization algorithms.

TASKZZ_GA/GA_Float/GA_Float.pde:57:float zero=-5; ///< Position Y==0 as coordinate in the window.
